## 5. Mapping the GDD to phases

| GDD / idea section | Phase |
|---|---|
| §1.2 camera and 4-layer cutaway | P0 (camera), P4 (layers) |
| §2 schedule and attendance | P1 |
| §3.1 status bars | P1 (heat), P2 (energy, HP), P5 (combat HP) |
| §3.2 attributes and training | P6 |
| §4 five prisons | P0–P8 (L1), P9–P12 |
| §5.1–5.3 cones, heat, bed dummy, disguise | P3 |
| §5.4–5.6 detectors, generator, cameras, snipers, solitary | P3 (solitary), P7 |
| §6 inventory and desks | P2 |
| §7 77 items | P2 (data), used by every later phase |
| §8 crafting and key cloning | P2 (crafting), P5 (molds) |
| §9 destruction, tunnels, concealment | P4 |
| §10 combat, KO, opinion, merchants, favors | P5, P6 |
| §11 jobs | P6 (L1), P9 (metalshop), P10 (all 6), P11 (janitor first) |
| §12 escape routes | P7 (L1), P9–P12 |
| §13 controls, HUD, feedback | Every phase, finished in P8 |

## 6. Tests

All tests are `scripts/*test*.ts` with golden snapshots (see the base playbook §5 and the sim
playbook §4):
- **Determinism**: same seed and same log run twice give the same state hash. A snapshot
  round trip gives the same hash.
- **Maps**: parse checksums and per-kind counts. Reachability per layer with capability sets.
  Zones, doors and props are present.
- **Data**: ids are unique, references resolve, the recipe graph is closed, INT gates can be
  reached, economy bounds hold, and French covers every key.
- **Mechanics**: tunnels, flushing, cave-ins, detectors, key cloning, combat formulas.
- **Bots**: regression bots (model prisoner, truant, smuggler, key thief, key cloner), plus
  one escape bot per prison and route that plays from day 1.
- **Performance**: the worst sim step with all NPCs stays under budget (yes/no in the
  snapshot, no timings).
- **Speech bubbles** (P13): the phrase banks in both languages (slots, voices, length,
  typography), the director over two days of play (variety, crowding, silence), and every
  piece of gossip checked against the running game in every prison.
- **Noise** (P14): how far each noise carries and who hears it, who is sent to look, every
  throwing rule, the firecracker (also saved and loaded mid-fuse), the alarm clock's timing,
  contraband over a detector arch, and the ear on the tool button proven equal to the sim.
- **Guard ways** (P15): each prison's traits and nap stops, the shakedown's cells known ahead
  and drawn as told, the naps (when, where, how long, what wakes them), looking around by trait,
  talking to guards and every bribe and refusal, and old saves.

## 7. Release routine

`npm run build && npm test`, then commit, `npm version minor|patch`, then
`git push --follow-tags origin main`, then poll the live bundle for the version. After that,
update the project memory and report what changed, what wasn't verified (sound, vibration,
real touch feel) and the known limits.

  - UI: the cash pill in the HUD (gold flash on income; since v2.6.1 the file's button, your face and cash) opening **your file** (I): attributes and where to train them, the job and today's quota, favors under way, followers; the routine pill counts the quota at work and says PAID; the job board; the talk panel (opinion meter with its 25/80 marks, the favor, the shop with friend prices, gifts, recruit); the training card (bar, alternating pads on touch, A/D on keys); machine progress chips; the floor arrows lead to the job room; sounds for coins, machines, reps, sets, purchases; coach tips for work, training, talking, favors;

- **P8+ step 3, architecture (v1.1.2)**, from an architecture review before P9–P12:
  - `src/sim/Sim.ts` split into system modules (2871 → 361 lines): `sim/state.ts` (the state, events and **one `freshState`** that both a new game and a loaded save start from, in field order), `sim/context.ts`, `systems/watch.ts`, `power.ts`, `justice.ts`, `fights.ts`, `actions.ts`, `jobs.ts`, `training.ts`, `social.ts`, `routine.ts`; `step()` is a flat list of calls in the old order. Proven identical: every snapshot unchanged, and the old and new sims side by side over 320,000 random steps (events, hashes, saves both ways);

  - the title's keys line breaks into two even lines in a rounded card, not a pill with a word left over.
- **P12 HMP Irongate (v1.5.0)**, see §2:
  - **HMP Irongate**: the map (64×64: the sea, a rocky shore, the outer wall and 4 towers, the walkway, the fortress: the north block with 14 cells, solitary, GEN-1 and the ladder; the open-air yard; the middle block; the main corridor; the south block with the workshops behind their arches, the control room, the sally port, the helipad court, GEN-2, the guard quarters; the dock and its pier; the ferry gate, the drawbridge and the landing), vents from cell 1 to GEN-1's ladder, 10 guards (4 riot), 8 cameras, 4 searchlights, 6 jobs (the woodshop's first), 86 pieces of decor (bollards, crates, rope, life rings, a windsock);
  - sim: `TILE.PIER`, the sea flooding tunnels (`systems/sea.ts`), two generators (`PrisonDef.grid`, `state.powerOff2`), the raft launched off the pier, the helicopter on its pad at night, grapnel landings across a chasm (`PrisonDef.landings`), the drawbridge (`PrisonDef.drawbridge`), riot guards (`GuardDef.riot`, `helipad`); new zones **helipad** and **dock** (staff only), escape routes **raft** and **helicopter**;
  - escape bots: the raft (keys, a pouch, GEN-2 off, the dock fence cut 25.7 s later), the helicopter (vents, roof, grapnel, rope, two cups of molten chocolate thrown unseen, aboard in uniform), the takeover (Strength 100, two followers, five cups kept for the riot guards, all ten out cold, out over the drawbridge); every job paid; `irongatetest`;
  - render: the island theme (`render/Island.ts`: the sea, shore, surf, boats, drawbridge, helicopter and the departures), riot guards, the helipad, the pier; sound: surf and gulls, the flood, the raft, the rotor; UI: the power pill names the dead half, labels (launch, board, under the sea, across the yard, GEN-1/GEN-2), the card art, help ("The island"), boat and helicopter icons, the campaign's end on the victory screen;
  - fixed on the way: the routine pass found the walk home from the yard too long (the cells' vestibule moved to the middle of the block), tiles boxed in by the quarters' table and the mailroom's shelf, the helipad guards walking through the parked helicopter.
- **P12 review (v1.5.1)**, a runtime pass (3 days × 4 seeds, a worker, an idle player) and the UI at desktop, 412×915 and 915×412, English and French:
  - a doorway between the library and the infirmary: a second way between the yard and the main corridor (the east side's workers walked round by the canteen; the latest at exercise from 26 to 21 minutes, nobody ever missing it);
  - the tunnel view shows the sea in unlit blue over the earth (out of the lantern's reach it was lost in the dark); the shallows fade out from the shore in one soft texture (they ended in a hard rectangle of tiles);
  - the touch tool button's verb takes two lines rather than an ellipsis ("Lancer le / grappin"; every verb in both languages checked); the raft's is "Cast off · on the raft" ("Larguer"), the helipad by day "No helicopter · until 23:00";
  - help: Irongate's topic as three short lines, one per way off (French "Intellect", the yard and the helipad court no longer both "la cour"); the campaign's end note in gold, kept clear of the buttons and on one line on landscape phones; the prison select's fifth card takes the whole row on landscape phones, the island card's sun clear of its number;
  - measured fine: no inmate misses a block, the idle player is locked down and caught, a step costs about 43 µs;
  - the takeover bot fights in the cell block's vestibule, a chokepoint (in the yard the last riot guard arrived when it had nothing left).
- **v1.5.3**: the prison select polished: on wide screens the five prisons side by side as posters (the world on top, the card below, the ways out lined up along the bottom), one highlight at a time (the card under the pointer, else the one focused), the ring drawn over the whole card (the scene hid it on phones), no second focus outline. v1.5.2 never went live: its Irongate takeover bot, rallying while roll call was still on, met every guard at once and lost (CI stopped the deploy); the bot now keeps the routine until 09:05 first.
- **v1.5.4**: every dialog in one dark style (`styles/14-dialogs.css`, loaded last): a steel-dark panel with a warm orange edge of light over the prison dimmed and blurred, glassy rows, orange for what's chosen, gold focus rings (one ring, never two); settings, how to play, saves (empty slots dashed), the tutorial, the prison select, pause (its links on one row), the question (an icon badge), the escape and the victory (the word in gold); the title menu's buttons in dark glass to match; long lists fade out at their foot.
- **P12 second review (v1.5.5)**: the helipad kept clear (solid): the player and the guards stood inside the parked helicopter; it is boarded from beside the pad (the bot, `irongatetest`); the in-game panels (desk, workbench, file, talk, job board) in the dialogs' finish (the orange edge of light, the steel grain, a hairline under the head); the file's attribute lines on two lines rather than cut off; checked: Irongate at night (lamps, surf, the dock's fence, the moored boat), French refusals and prompts at 412 px.
- **Haptics felt on Android (v1.5.6)**: every vibration pulse at least 30 ms (the 6–10 ms ticks never spun the motor up: nothing was felt on a real phone); the strong patterns longer. iPhones have no web vibration (the setting stays hidden there).
- **Talking to someone seated (v1.5.7)**: a seat or bed gives way to a seated inmate the player can talk to, either on the spot the player would take or in front of them (within 45°) more squarely than that spot. Before, facing someone at the canteen from a diagonal or a little off-centre offered only Sit (refused: seat taken). Talking to inmates on the move is unchanged (user decision). `livingtest`: the diagonal canteen case (fails on the old rule).
- **Full review, AAA polish (v1.6.0)**: render: contact shadows under everyone (one instanced draw, every tier: the lowest has no shadow map), baked occlusion at the foot of every furniture and building model (`kit.merge`; characters and items left as painted), soft patches over the open ground breaking the one-tile repeat (baked into the contact-shadow layer), calmer grass, road, gravel and wall-top speckle, window glass lit at night (door panes, tower cabins; dark with the power out), a CSS lens vignette (no GPU pass). UI: the last light surfaces in the dark family (the update card, which waits at the top on the title; the item toast over an open panel; carousel arrows), the round action verb on two lines rather than cut off, an orange focus ring on slots and help tabs. Checked: French at 412×915 with touch emulated (nothing cut off), 60 fps at a 4× CPU slowdown (167 draw calls), day and night. Fog was looked at and left out: an orthographic camera has no depth across the screen for it to show.
- **The update toast (v1.6.1)**: on a phone it was a tall, narrow strip (`left: 50%` alone caps an absolutely placed box at half the screen, so the text wrapped line after line). Now centred by both edges, at most 480 px wide: an orange icon tile, "Update ready" with "Version x · restart to play it" under it, a compact Restart; at the top on the title, at the bottom elsewhere. Checked at 412×915, 915×412, in English and French.
- **v1.6.2**: no change in the game; released so a phone on v1.6.1 is offered an update and shows the new toast (each version draws its own offer: a phone on v1.6.0 still showed the old strip announcing 1.6.1).
- **The other four prisons' visuals (v1.6.3)**: Irongate's sea redrawn (a 256 px seamless swell texture at three scales, smooth and lower contrast, repeating every 20 tiles rather than every 4.6: it read as a grid of hard blobs), the surf soft froth instead of white static; Shankton's crushed stone calmer (it read as noise); Stalag Flucht's snow-capped wall tops a cooler grey-white against the snow, softer road ruts; San Pancho's raked minefield lines softer; Irongate's turf and granite caps calmer. Checked each in daylight at the widest zoom.
- **Polish leftovers (v1.6.4)**: softer shadow edges per tier (PCF filter radius 3 texels on high, 1.6 on medium; three r186 dropped PCFSoftShadowMap, its PCF takes the radius at the same 5 samples); auto quality steps back up one tier after 40 s at 56+ fps (never above the device's starting tier; if the raised tier runs slow again it steps down and stays). Fog stays out (orthographic camera); the title backdrop already drifted (since P5).
- **The new version, "Prison Life" (decided 2026-10-16)**: the user asked for fun features beyond the clone and chose all of them: noise and distractions, daily events and weather, guard dogs and a pet rat, guards with personalities, mood speech bubbles ("varied, never repetitive or linear"), new ways out (stowaway, fake-ID bluff, glider), smuggling, crew specialists, side games, achievements and medals, your prisoner, escape replays and a Daily Escape. Everywhere in the campaign, tuned: the 18 escape bots keep winning, byte for byte. One release at a time (P13 Voices → P14 Noise → P15 Guard ways → P16 Achievements & you → P17 The day → P18 Stowaway → P19 Dogs → P20 Rat → P21 Smuggling → P22 Games → P23 Crew → P24 Bluff & glider → P25 Replays → P26 Daily Escape as v2.0).
- **P13 Voices (v1.7.0)**: speech bubbles over inmates and guards (see §2): HTML over the 3D view (no draw call), at most 4 on a desktop, 3 on a phone held sideways, 2 upright, never over the HUD or the controls (measured), nudged apart; the director picks who speaks by voice, mood and distance, a fitting line not heard lately (now and then from another mood that fits the moment), and stays silent rather than repeat. 1,000 lines a language in 174 banks; 21 kinds of gossip, each checked against the running game in every prison (bed checks observed, the generator switched off, a tunnel dug past its braces, guards floored until the takeover, a bed check with and without a dummy). Guard names on the target card ("Officer Pratt · cyan key"); the inmate's greeting in the talk panel; a Rumors section in the file. The bed checks' times moved into `tuning.ts` (`BED_CHECK_MINUTES`) so the gossip quotes the rule's own numbers. Measured: 60 fps under a 4× CPU slowdown with bubbles up; two days of play say about 300 lines, 91%+ distinct in any 100, none again among the last 40.
- **P13 review (v1.7.1)**: a code review and a runtime pass (San Pancho's roll call, a canteen at every meal, a fight, a lockdown, night in the cells, the talk panel and the file, desktop and both phone sizes, English and French). Fixed: a bubble never covers the player (it slides aside with its tail on its speaker, or glides up over the one in the way; stacked bubbles lose their tail), sits clear of the "!" and "$" badges, fades out, pops in once; a line that never finds room on screen is let go after 0.8 s, and only lines actually seen play the murmur, reach the screen reader (queued, a few seconds apart) or put gossip in the notebook; a guard's reason for spotting the player (any reaction of weight) takes the place of an idle line instead of being lost; an exchange starts only with room for both lines and its reply names the same guard or meal; the talk partner's answers (a gift, a favor paid, joining up) show in the talk panel (bubbles are hidden while it's open); greetings keep their own memory, a grudge shows before the shop's welcome, and the friendly greeting waits for the panel's own "friend" band (80); gossip names its teller (the bubble and the file); merchants are only recommended by gossip, and only name their wares, when they would sell; GEN-1 at Irongate (detectors and cameras) no longer sets off blackout talk; nothing waits through sleep (no 04:00 bed-check line at 07:55); sleep-talk reaches the next cells (7 tiles), now and then; night guards keep to night lines; lines tied to the moment (morning, free time, the first two days, indoors) wait for it; "all 5 guards" instead of "5 of the 5" (`takeoverAll`); the three soap jokes replaced (family-friendly); French places no longer read "à le bloc"; saved rumors are checked field by field.
- **Talk panel buttons (v1.7.2)**: "Une astuce ?" no longer wraps in two when its note is long (the button shrank to fit the note). The tip and recruiting rows share one grid: the buttons are as wide as the widest, each label on one line, the notes lined up beside them and wrapping instead; the favor's button keeps its label on one line too. Checked in every state (tip ready, needed, used; recruit ready, needed; following) at desktop, 412×915 and 915×412, in English and French.
- **P14 Noise & distractions (v1.8.0)**: guards hear the player's work and anything thrown, by the rules in §2: a noise spreads tile by tile where sight would pass (a shut door costs 4, walls stop it; a wall being chipped is heard on both sides), from below only on the 3×3 tiles above, from the vents within 2 tiles below, from a roof on the open ground around (off the ground floor a tile's cost is its distance across and down, so the guards sent walk towards it); the guards awake within earshot hear it, the nearest go and look where it came from (`src/sim/systems/noise.ts`: `makeNoise`, `hearers`, `wouldHear`, `strikeNoise`, `aimToss`, `throwItem`, `noisemakers` first in the step). Throwing (`OP.EXT`, the first of the shared extension ops: `EXT.THROW`, the ground tile aimed at in its two arguments, so a throw sent while walking lands where it was aimed; commands gained a third 6-bit argument): on a mouse, hold the right button to aim and let go (or Throw in the item's menu, then click; F straight ahead); on touch, Throw in the item's menu (or the firecracker's own button), drag and lift. The aim shows the reach, a dotted arc, the landing ring, the tiles the thud would reach and an ear over each guard who would hear it; a label says who would (or why it can't go), kept clear of the hint and the screen's edges. Lands at once (the view flies it, the item waits for it to land), spills off a full pile, −2 opinion with an inmate it hits, never scanned over an arch. The firecracker lands lit (`state.fuses`, saved) and goes off 1.5 s later: a bang that wakes the guards asleep within earshot and sends three. The alarm clock is wound with the use button (20 s a press, a minute at most; a press stops it ringing), counts down wherever it is (its slot holds its state: in the pockets, furniture, someone's pockets, the floor) and rings once a second for 8 s; its countdown shows on its slot, updated in place (a redraw every second would eat clicks). An ear on the tool button (and the Space prompt) when a guard would hear the work. Waves and the reached tiles flash where a noise is made; a "?" over each guard sent to look; sounds (clatter soft/hard/metal, the fuse, the bang, the ring, a tick in the pockets, winding); bubble lines for guards and inmates (8 new banks). A lesson, "Noise" (tutorial 9 of 10); a lesson step can now end the lesson when it can't be done any more (both firecrackers gone off out of earshot: try again). An independent review found 10 bugs before release (the lesson's dead end, the hint over the phone buttons, a throw on the move landing a tile on, the label under the hint or the item bar, toasts over the hint, guards stalling on a roof or vent noise, clicks lost to the clock's redraws, the ear left on the attack button, a lit fuse behind the title, ticking ×10 while out cold): all fixed, the lesson's and the throw's with tests. Escape bots unchanged; at most +4 draw calls while aiming; 60 fps at a 4× CPU slowdown.
- **P15 Guard ways (v1.9.0)**: guards have their ways (`GuardDef.trait`, §2): steady, lazy, strict, corrupt, set per prison (Center Perks: Pratt corrupt, Dunn strict, Moss lazy, napping in the yard). A lazy guard dozes off at its night round's nap stop (`Waypoint.nap`) between 01:00 and 05:00, once a night, for as long as `roll()` says: blind (no cone, `awake()` false), folded arms and Z's, mumbling in its sleep; it wakes to a noise that reaches it with 2 tiles to spare (any bang in earshot: `noise.ts`, and the ear on the tool button agrees), to the player at its elbow for a second (it comes to facing them), a lockdown, a job. Looking around runs down by trait (a lazy guard ×0.75, a strict one ×1.5, worked out from the step); a strict guard searches three cells at the shakedown, each desk for 6 s. `state.salt` (the seed mixed; an old save mixes its rng) and `roll()`/`rollInt()` in `rng.ts` give the newer rules their randomness without touching `state.rng`: the shakedown's cells come from it (`shakedownCells`), the two old draws still made. Talking to a guard (`s.talk` holds a guard's Cast id; `canChat`): it stands still facing the player; its panel shows its name, its key, its ways once a rumor told them, a greeting in its voice, and two bribes (`TALK.BRIBE`, `src/sim/systems/ways.ts`): look the other way (2 game hours; `watch.ts` lets the hour, staff areas, contraband and a uniform pass under its eyes, and its sight doesn't count as seen) and lose the paperwork (−25 heat, once a day); a strict guard reports the offer (+20 heat), the others say no; refused at 80% heat, a look away off at 80%. A HUD chip counts down the look away; the bribed guard's cone turns pale green. Gossip tells each guard's ways (and where and from when a lazy one naps) and which cells the next shakedown will search, all checked by `gossiptest` against the game (each guard offered a bribe, the naps and shakedowns that happened). Guard voices by trait (slacker, stickler, crook; steady guards stay rookies or veterans), 15 new banks (dozing, bribed, greetings, a guard woken, bribes taken, refused, reported). Help topic "Guard ways"; coach hints for talking to a guard and for the first dozing one seen. An independent review found 8 issues before release, all fixed: a dozing guard could be beaten out cold without waking (a blow wakes it now, a knockout ends the nap); a guard's ways fell out of the notebook as daily shakedown news came in (lasting rumors are always kept, news capped at 12); the look away's countdown redrew the panel every second and disarmed the paperwork button (now written in place); dozing guards shouted reactions; a talk could hold a guard past its block (it ends when its duty changes); the paperwork limit is once a day per guard (said so); a strict shakedown's last search may run into breakfast (documented). Escape bots unchanged; 184 draw calls, 60 fps.
- **P15 review (v1.9.1)**: three independent reviews (rules, UI and text, 3D) and a pass in the browser (desktop, 412×915 and 915×412, English and French). Rules: a talk begun the very step a guard came back from looking around held it with no end (its duty not planned yet, so Sim's `dutyDue` never fired: the freeze now waits for a planned duty); a bribe offered the step the player became wanted was dropped without a word (refused, "too hot", nothing paid or reported); every nap's end is told once (`nap` off), whatever ended it (a lockdown, a chase, a job, a look, a knockout: 12 of 45 naps ended silently in a 3-day fuzz). 3D: the dozing guard's Z's rose from inside its head (a sleeper's height) and outlived its nap: they now float from over its cap, belong to the guard (one at a time, fading with the doze, still under reduced motion, in the in-bed Z's Fredoka); the doze eases in and out (arms unfold on waking, a little start with the head jerking up), the chin sinks and jerks back up (a nod-off), eyes shut; a "$" pops over a guard as it takes the money; the bribed cone is emerald (apart from the cameras' cyan), eased, breathing, and pulses back toward yellow over the last 10 game minutes; Z's cleared on a new prison. UI: the look away reads "until 19:05" (the HUD clock's time) in the panel and the chip (h:mm read as minutes and seconds), the chip's last minutes in red, its time no longer flooding screen readers; a guard's ways show once found out first-hand (it took the money: corrupt; it reported you: strict), or always with the chatter off (nobody gossips then); known not to take money, the pay buttons dim and the warning goes; a guard robbed of its key says so; the pay buttons name what they buy; key rings in the world's key colours; the nap hint waits for the guard to be in sight; heat as a percentage everywhere; French gossip lines start with a capital ("Le gardien Pratt…"), "Parler au gardien Pratt", "Payer 50 $ ?", clearer bribe wording and help; the strict chip's own icon (a siren); `.big-btn.small` 44 px tall (touch). `traittest` gained the three rule fixes. Escape bots unchanged.
- **P15 second review (v1.9.2)**: the same three reviews again, the first review's fixes included, and a night in the browser (a real nap at 01:21). Rules: the morning shakedown's toast named two cells while every prison's strict shakedown guard searches three ("cells 6, 8 and 4": `listOf`), and the help said "two cells" (now two or three, three by a strict guard); no talking to a guard in a guard's uniform (`canChat`: face to face it only ever ended in a lockdown 4 s later). Presentation: a strict guard's report that makes the player wanted ends the talk in the same step, so its ways were never learned (the talk partner now comes from the events); first-hand facts read "you found out" in the file (an eye, not an ear); once a guard's ways are known the panel says what to expect ("Reports a bribe: +20% heat", "Won't take your money"); a player short of cash can still make the offer (dimmed: the guard answers, a strict one reports it; the "can't afford me" lines were never heard); "Le gardien Pratt ferme les yeux" (a French subject with its article; the chip gives way with an ellipsis on a narrow phone); the touch action tag opens with a capital; no "night shift, right" from a guard woken by the sirens or a chase; text fixes (a repeated eyelid joke, "Keep it short", "Off you go", "offense", "Que je suis maladroit", the help on a look away ending when wanted and on learning ways first-hand). 3D: going from calm to spotted no longer eases yellow → red through orange (alarms snap; calm and looking away ease); a cone fading out (dozing, out cold) keeps its colour instead of drifting to yellow; a brief flare as a cone comes back on; no "$" over a guard floored while looking into something; the "$" settles small over a paid guard and stays (hidden under a doze's Z's), darker green (4.7:1); the standing Z's drift up and right on screen whatever way the guard faces (they stacked into one under the steep camera), a little larger; a dozing guard stirs visibly before waking (Z's fade, the chin lifts, eyelids flutter: the player is at its elbow), eyes wide for a moment on the start; a wider sway; the bribed cone's breathing visible; the Z texture drawn again once the web font has loaded. `traittest`: no talk in uniform, a report into a chase. Escape bots unchanged.
- **P16 Achievements & you (v1.10.0)**: a run ledger in the sim (§2), counted after each step from its events (counters for blows, knockouts, throws, decoys, bangs, caught, solitary, lockdowns, missed blocks, spotted, the heat's peak, buys, crafts, wages, favors, chocolate, meals, dummy passes (the bed check says so), power cuts, tapes, bribes, mishaps, potatoes, tiles walked…; per-day bits); 42 achievements as data (`content/achievements.ts`), 33 of them giving a cosmetic; medals by day per way out (`content/medals.ts`, Center Perks tunnel 2/4/7 … Irongate takeover 8/12/16); records v2 keep each way out's quickest and medal (migrated: a prison escaped one way only gets its quickest and medal; older escapes earn what they show, never conduct); lifetime stats (`pe3d.stats`), trophies (`pe3d.trophies`), your prisoner (`pe3d.prisoner`, kept on reset). The trophy shelf (Trophies | Medals | Stats) from the title's new third button (a dot when something is new); the gold unlock card with a fanfare, read out through one live region; the Intake on the first game (skippable) and from the shelf: the 3D preview stands in the cell behind the title, face on, turned by dragging, beside a panel (a bottom sheet on a phone upright). The victory card shows the medal (or how quick bronze needed to be) and what the escape earned. The escape camera replaces the fixed pauses (the HUD steps away meanwhile; the zoom comes back after). Run logs next to each save (copied with it, so two branches from one save make two whole logs), the last escape's and each prison's quickest; checksums of a stable fingerprint. Reviewed by two independent passes before release: hair under the guard cap, hats sitting on bald and buzz-cut heads, three coplanar faces, the zoom after an escape (and an older bug: each new game's zoom compounded), the stamp and toast for screen readers, the skip that kept turning, the name field rebuilding the preview, tab panels and arrow keys in the Intake, a save made room for by dropping logs, an escape counted once per run, a damaged ledger loading partial. Verified in the browser at desktop, 412×915 and 915×412, English and French (shelf, Intake, toast, escape and card); cosmetics add no draw call (290 either way in the same view).
- **P16 review (v1.10.1)**: three independent reviews (rules and saves, UI and text, 3D) and a pass in the browser. Rules: a replay never checked the end of a run (the escape is there, after the last hourly checksum): a log now ends on a checksum of its last step and a replay is whole only if it gets there with every checksum met; Pacifist went to runs that knocked guards out by throwing chocolate or through followers (it now needs no blow, nothing flung at anyone, nobody knocked out; new counters `flung` and `crew`, appended); Popular counted one inmate recruited twice (it is now two followers at once); a full storage could lose the autosave while slot logs and best replays stayed (every log gives way first; the autosave is deleted before an escape's log is kept, and a new quickest replaces the old quickest's log even when its own can't be kept); career achievements waited for the title (the stats catch up every half minute in play). UI: the unlock card was squeezed to half a phone's width (centred by both edges now), drew over dialogs (it waits under them), the landscape victory card hid its buttons with a medal, achievements and a prison opened (two columns then, the chips one sliding line); the Intake as tabs and a radio group (arrows switch part, arrows choose; one Tab stop each), its panel no longer jumping between parts (the picture follows it), Escape in the name only leaves the name, Escape before a first game goes back to the title; achievements read as earned or not, medals named not by colour alone ("Gold by day 3"), progress bars named, a mystery read as hidden; bigger tabs and close button on phones; French text ("Dossier : Anna", "Change ton look", "Cheveux", unbreakable hyphens, "$500"), Clean Record and Pacifist reworded, the help says Know-how, ??? mishaps and where medals' days are. 3D: the bandana, bucket hat, chef's hat and goggles strap no longer share faces with the hair (flicker); the straw hat and top hat cover the hair under their brims; the headband sits round the hair, not in it, and stays on the brow; hats sit a hair above a buzz cut or a bald head; a mohawk under a hat is a buzz cut, curls keep their sides and back; the short cut's top no longer shares the face's front; the cuffs' trim sits proud of the sleeve; the escape camera never pulls back past the zoom limit, its shadows cover the turned view's corners, and it follows the raft or the helicopter; the Intake's camera eases in and out (at once with reduced motion); the preview prisoner has a contact shadow.
- **P17 The day (v1.11.0)**: every prison day has a plan (§2), pure and known ahead: what the warden says, what inmates whisper about tomorrow and what happens are the same thing. Sim: `systems/calendar.ts` (`dayPlan`, `today`, `theDay` on each minute, `dayWatch` after the guards look, `sightOf`, `muffled`, `crowdPlan`), state `calendar` and `eventFlags` appended (an old save's day stays quiet), `cutPower` shared by the player, storms and the service, ledger counters `commended`, `mustered`, `drillsMissed`, `feasts`, `foodBlows`, `baked`; 8 achievements in a new group, The day (Teacher's Pet, Present and Correct, Seconds Please, Food Fighter, Storm Runner, Pea Souper; Sunstroke and Drill Dodger hidden). Play: the warden's morning card (a greeting in their voice, typed; the event and the weather with what they change), announcement strips that wait for banners, the chime and a radio voice; a Today chip under the HUD (coming, on, the drill's muster draining), the weather by the clock, the drill in the routine pill, the heatwave in the cold's chip, the file's Today (and Tomorrow after the forecast); lightning and thunder, rain and wind sound; inmates and guards talk about the day (22 banks), four day rumours (tomorrow's inspection, drill, food fight; tonight's storm cut), all proven true in gossiptest. View: rain streaks and ripples on hard ground, a fog veil clear round the player, storm and heat light, wind in the snow, the film on a screen over the canteen, food flying in arcs, two inspectors trailing a guard; cones as long as each guard sees. Reviewed by three independent passes before release (rules and saves, UI and text, 3D): the inspection's blots counted a block missed before it and missed one missed during it, and ignored what was already being noticed at 10:00 (the commendation now comes after the last step's blots); a throw kept a punch's food-fight pass (a guard knocked out for nothing); Irongate's storm skipped GEN-2 when GEN-1 was already off; a longer cut sent the generator's guard away early; the drill now excuses anyone out cold, in solitary or in a lockdown at any point of the muster (and roll call isn't where to be in a lockdown); a heatwave in the snow counts as the sun's; the warden's line no longer vanishes under a banner, waits for the morning card, closes with Esc, gives toasts room in landscape; no lightning in a blizzard; the drill chip red only at the end, a Missed badge, nothing pending when excused; the file says what's over, a forecast drill "that day", a food fight unannounced; "an hour" in the sun (a game hour is a real minute); the fog icon only while the fog lies thick; French fixes; the inspectors on a steady 30 Hz trail; rain and ripples at the right height from the roofs and vents; the fog under every floor marker; no allocation per frame; the screen only near the canteen, over its wall; the rain box covering the view; no weather carried into the next game; cones easing to a new length.
- **P17 review (v1.11.1)**: three independent reviews again (rules and saves, UI and text with a browser pass at the three sizes in both languages, 3D) and a pass in the browser. Rules: a clean inspection paid nothing to a player out cold at 16:00; a free food-fight blow hid tools at work, damage found, contraband and cameras for its moment (scuffles now never mask them, and a food fight's blows are noticed not at all); the drill mustered a player out cold or hunted on the roll-call tiles as the bell rang; Food Fighter counted any blow from the canteen (now the free ones, throws too, carried on the event); a Daily twist could make day 1 foggy; logs from before the day replay with every day quiet, and an old log is never carried on under the new rules. UI: the warden's clock is play time (paused, nothing runs out or starts; Esc goes to the pause card, not a card behind it); a line cut short by a banner comes back without a second chime or reading, or not at all once stale; the card goes under the first day's guide; the file keeps tomorrow after a reload, says "Tonight" and "From 08:00" after midnight (the HUD's day has turned), tells a food fight or drill under way as it is, and keeps its notes on short screens; the landscape card says every rule in full; French wind ("de l’ouest"), "Toucher pour fermer", fog no longer said to muffle noise, the inspection's extra tile capped by the weather outdoors, truer bubbles. 3D: the fog lies over the yard and thins indoors (an outdoor mask), under the floor's paint, shadows and puddles but over nothing that would give a guard away, leaves the tunnels at once, never seams the film; snow falls in front of it; rain no longer streaks across cut-away rooms and covers the whole view at any zoom; the weather's light eases in and out and lets the lockdown's red through; the inspectors stay out of walls, ease onto their spots and keep to their guard from the vents and roofs; the film dims in a power cut; the day's shaders compile as a game starts; fewer allocations, a single-pass beam, no film mipmaps.
- **P17 second review (v1.11.2)**: three more independent reviews (rules and saves with probes against v1.10.1, UI and text in the browser at the three sizes in both languages, 3D). Rules: a food fight's free blows reach its doorways and the corridor tiles it opens onto (a punch across the edge cost a lockdown); a wall chipped from the yard is muffled by rain and storms like the yard; solitary served into an inspection blots it; runs logged before 1.11 replay with their scuffles judged as they were then (`Sim.legacy`, not state). Logs that 1.11.0 carried on from a 1.10 game can't be told apart and may verify only up to their first event day (P25 shows what verified). UI: the day-1 welcome waits for the roll call's banner, and any banner over the morning card hides it and holds its clock; announcements switched off drop what's queued too; the warden's board pushes the combat target card down; the card stays up as long as it takes to read; typing runs on play time; the Today chip reads a whole phrase to screen readers (never the countdown), the live lines use French punctuation; the warden's voice follows the Voices setting and never plays after a pause; the drill chip's last seconds don't blink with reduced motion. 3D: fog over the roofs when you're up there; free-standing walls no longer part the fog; the day's shaders compile again after a graphics change (phones step down mid-game); cones grow at once and ease only when they shrink (never shorter than the guard sees); the inspectors wait by their guard if it's knocked out instead of jumping across the map; the lockdown's red pulses in the fog, dimmer at night; the film's frame drawn over the fog; rain, the fog's clear patch and its mask follow the escape camera's turn; ripples across the whole view; a game loaded in fog starts foggy.
- **P18 Delivery day (v1.12.0)**: a weekly vehicle and the stowaway's way out (§2). Sim: `systems/delivery.ts` (`vehicleIn`, `thorough`, `deliveryDuty`, `atTail`, `stow`, `climbOut`, `deliveryMinute`, `clearBay`), `World.setDelivery` (`parked` solid and opaque, `propped` doors), the supervisor's `PLAN_DELIVERY` round and `PLAN_SIGN` post, inmates never pick a spot under the vehicle, the hidden player out of every guard's sight, blows, grudges, the cold and attendance; state `stowed` and `stowCaught` appended; `ESCAPES` += `stowaway`, `DAY_EVENTS` += `delivery`, `SALT.DELIVERY`; ledger `stowed`, `slipped`, `stowFound`; achievements Special Delivery, Off the Books, Air Mail and Return to Sender (hidden); medal rows. Play: the morning card's row (the vehicle, when, who searches), warnings, the sign-off and search lines, the HUD chip, a hidden card (where, when it leaves, the search, Wait), Hide in / Climb out on the action button, a found banner; truthful rumours (tomorrow's delivery, how thorough today's search is); bubbles as it comes in, at the search, at a stowaway found. View: the trucks back in along their road (the gates on the way opening, reversing beeps), their tailgates open and shut, a ring at the tail drawn over everything, the vehicle fading while the player is round its far side, the departure followed by the camera; Irongate's helicopter sets down by day with its ramp down.
- **P18 review (in v1.12.0)**: three independent reviews before the release (rules and saves, UI and text in the browser at both phone sizes, 3D). Rules: damage the guards found while the player hid cost nothing (now the alarm goes up all the same, and its lockdown searches the vehicle); at Center Perks (and Irongate's court) the propped doors shut at 14:31 on a player still out past them (they now stay open that day until the player is back, worked out from the state); Interact at the tail after the search did nothing while the button offered something else (no climbing in once the tailgate is shut); climbing out on the very step it leaves no longer counts as away with it; after a catch every search is thorough for two weeks (3 days hardly ever met the next weekly delivery); the search's outcome kept in the day's flags (a game loaded after it says how it went); an old save made the evening before a delivery keeps tomorrow's old card too (the forecast named it); the quick-look rumour says "unless the sirens go". UI: the hidden card and veil cleared on leaving a game; the bubbles go on while hidden (the search's words are for the stowaway); "Hide in the supply truck"; "Not with the guards after you"; no double announcement when found (the capture's banner says where); the search's outcome to screen readers; the Wait button's one name; only E, Space, I and H while hidden (no craft panel, no lock-on); the card clear of a short desktop window's key prompt; the sign-off says both 14:30 and 14:31; Irongate's stowaway route shows the helicopter. 3D: the tail's arrow points into the vehicle; the wheels roll back as it reverses; its shaders compile with the day's; the ring on the ground floor only; gates on its road stay open while it's on them, paused too; nothing carried into the next game, the wheels' buffer freed; one engine at the escape; steady lamps with reduced motion; the helicopter's ramp slides out from under the cabin (no longer through the boom at night); the truck opaque unless faded.
- **P19 Guard dogs (v1.13.0)**: four guard dogs at their handlers' heels (§2). Sim: `systems/k9.ts` runs after the watch: shifts from day 2 (10:00–22:00, nights 01:00–05:00 for Brutus, Bandido and Admiral, every lockdown), the nose (contraband in the hotbar, 1.5 tiles, a pouch blocks it, the uniform doesn't), the player's trail out of bounds (32 tiles, 90 s) and dogs following it to the player or to the way they went (covers fool no dog: damage found, the alarm), jerky, pepper and sleepy jerky thrown to a dog, soap and showers; daily treats on the canteen counters and pills in a `roll()`-chosen desk; `SPOT` += `sniffed`, `SALT.TREATS`, ledger `sniffed`, `dogDug`, `dogsFed`, `dogsPeppered`, `dogsAsleep`, `dogFriends`, `washed`; achievements Good Boy, Bless You, Sweet Dreams, Squeaky Clean, Nosy Parker (hidden). Play: the paw in the heat pill (a dog close by would smell your contraband; amber) and while one is on your trail (red), proven on before every sniff; Throw to <dog> on the tool button with a treat in hand; Wash off with soap; toasts and sounds (barks by size, sneezes, munching, a yawn, scrubbing); the handlers' lines, inmates' at a dog fed; rumours of the dogs' hours, what works on them and whose desk has the pills; the warden introduces the dogs on day 2. View: low-poly dogs in four coats (trotting, sitting, nose down on the trail, pointing with sound rings, sneezing, lying by the kennel, asleep with Z's), kennels with a bowl, the trail's amber shoe prints and a tracking dog's paw prints. Escape bots unchanged (dogs start on day 2).
- **P19 review (in v1.13.0)**: three independent reviews before the release (rules and saves with probes, UI and text, 3D with measured bounds). Rules: a dog went on tracking (and raised the alarm) with its handler knocked out (now it leaves the trail); a trail it couldn't follow was picked up and lost four times a second in a lockdown (a 10 s rest after losing one); a grate or shaft merely beside a trail's end was "found" when the player had just walked back in bounds (only where the trail marks the player leaving the floor); the paw could miss a sniff on the step a dog came on shift, woke or stopped sneezing (that step it only gathers itself); treats were stocked in runs logged before the dogs; a treat fed a dog round a corner (a clear line now); the daily stock never overwrites what someone stashed; dog rumours wait for day 2; the shower no longer washes (and toasts) every step for a wanted player; a find by a dog is damage like any other (put right after a capture). UI: one dog's introduction in the singular; a dog finding the way out says so (not "sniffed you out"); gender-free French for Canela; "contraband pouch"; per-dog limits on the trail toasts ("lost" only after "on your trail"); the hints come before the cost; the paw said to screen readers; "would smell"; a greyed button for a dog fed today; "Throw at" for pepper; the dog's name on the touch button; help text with the alarm and where the treats are. 3D: the kennel's gable under its roof; the lying dog in front of its kennel, tail curled; sitting and lying on the floor (no paws under it, no floating rump); paws on the ground at a trot; no shear as a broad dog's head turns; prints keep their side and read in daylight; Z's above a sleeping head; bark rings cleared between prisons and a still ring with reduced motion.
- **P19 second review (v1.13.1)**: three more independent reviews (rules with probes, UI and text in the browser, 3D measured). Rules: a dog could smell a player on the very step they came back onto the ground floor (out of the vents, a vehicle, solitary, a faint) with no paw before it (a dog's nose now works on a player already about as the step began); a dog's find while the player was out cold or in solitary marked a hole found but raised nothing, so no guard ever would (left be now, as the guards leave damage then); the trail's "left the floor here" mark could land on an older tile (only on the tile the player stood on as the step began); a capture washes the chase's trail off; a dog picking up a trail no longer loses it at once for a stale wait; jerky goes to a dog in reach that hasn't had its share. A bribed handler's dog still smells and barks (a dog isn't bribed); a find by a dog is damage like any other (put right after a capture, or when the hunt gives up). UI: the sleepy jerky recipe in the handbook and tips where there are dogs (their treats now count as obtainable); soap's Wash off only where there are dogs; the help topic generic and shown once Shankton is open (no spoilers); "Trail washed off, but out of bounds you're already leaving a new one"; a dog finding the way out in a lockdown says so; the greyed Throw to button says why and keeps the jerky; first-time hints for treats and pills; "One jerky a day per dog"; the warden's joke in French and for one dog; a dog finding the player somewhere allowed is only a toast; the paw held a moment at the edge of its reach; the prisoner's file lists the dogs (trust, today's jerky); Good Boy's bone icon. 3D: Brutus's kennel moved off Shankton's lockers (k9test now checks decor and the floor in front of every kennel); dogs placed exactly where the sim has them (no lag, no glide) and never inside their kennel; feet on the floor through every pose blend, toes clear at a trot; head and tail ease between poses; the lying dog and its tail clear of the kennel; prints alternate on diagonals and shrink away; a broader roof with a raised ridge; bark rings only on the ground floor; nose lower on the trail.
- **P20 The pet rat (v1.14.0)**: a rat trap, a caught rat, a tame pet on the player's shoulder (§2). Sim: `systems/rats.ts` after the dogs (traps checked at 04:00 by `roll()`, `SALT.TRAPS`; errands along the rat's own way; panics that scatter inmates and draw guards; `SALT.SCURRY` for its darting), `wokeKeyless` last in the step (a guard woken without his key: a spare and the alarm), dogs chase rats (`DOG.RAT`), inmates flee a loose rat; ledger `ratsCaught`, `ratsTamed`, `ratFetched`, `ratKeys`, `ratLeft`, `ratPanics`, `keyHeists`, `keyless`; achievements Pied Piper, Rat Mail, Rat Race, Inside Job, Rude Awakening (hidden). Play: the rat's pill under the HUD (its name, out / coming back / loose with a countdown / waiting) opens its panel (T on a keyboard): what it's doing, Let it loose (with the people it would scare, or why not), the places in reach nearest first (furniture by room or whose cell, sleepers and the knocked out) with Fetch and Leave; Feed the rat · n/2 and Set down · 60% by 04:00 on the tool button; toasts, squeaks and scurries; inmates shriek, guards grumble, a guard shouts for his key; rumours of where to trap rats, what a guard does without his key, dogs and rats; help topic and key rows. View: a low-poly rat perched on the right shoulder (tail down the back), scurrying on its errands with what it carries in its teeth, darting about loose, hidden in the ducts over walls; the trap and the caught rat as items. Escape bots unchanged.
- **P20 review (in v1.14.0)**: three independent reviews before the release (rules with probes, UI and text in the browser, 3D measured). Rules: a guard with stuffed pockets missed his key every morning (he now draws the spare, an odd item dropped, and misses it once); inmates who fled a panic stood idle on their seats until the block ended (they take up what they were doing; only those in sight of the rat flee); the rat stranded after a capture (it now finds the player anywhere its way reaches, and says so when there's none); the key alarm now goes up before the doors are settled; Inside Job only for a key really missing from a sleeping guard's pockets; old saves' errands to containers gone; a loose rat never cuts through a wall; a dog's chase ends with its handler out cold; runs logged before 1.14 replay without the new key rule and aren't carried on. UI: the rat's panel never stacks over a talk, the board, the workbench or a container, and closes when the game can't take it (asleep, out cold, quitting); its status a live region written in place; sleepers listed first, up to 12 places with "n more" past them, each with its distance and compass point; a guard's key chip only while the key is on him, with a text alternative; T puts the focus in the panel and Space presses its buttons; "they're up" says the rat brings back what it couldn't leave; one toast for a night's catch, none repeated on the rat's return; "waits for you" when it can't come back; a 40 px pill on touch; French agreements (déposé là-bas : la clé), cantine and remise as the zones say, pomme de terre, K.-O., no-break spaces; help: which tools are small, napping guards wake by 05:00. 3D: the perched rat marks the stencil (the player's see-through silhouette no longer paints it), sits on the body's shoulder (no sinking into a swinging arm), turns with the player as drawn, its tail thick at the root and drooping; floor rats ease round corners and glide across errand changes; the trap's cup tipped over the bait on its matchstick, the caught rat's tail whole on the board; darker fur that reads on light floors; rats seen from the roofs too.
- **P20 second review (v1.14.1)**: three more independent reviews (rules with probes, UI and text in the browser, 3D measured). Rules: an inmate fleeing a panic keeps its spot (its goal is no longer hidden: seats and the delivery's bay see it) and takes up what it was doing there on its return without a fresh draw; a guard knocked out with stuffed pockets draws his spare too (an odd item dropped), so he's never missed twice; a dog rests 10 s after chasing a rat (no bark and guards every 5 s) and runs nose down; a loose rat darts only across the floor (never through a ducted wall into the next room); a save's errand to nowhere waits. UI: T puts the focus on the first Fetch (not Close), and a rebuild keeps the focus on the same button; the screen reader hears the rat's state once, not a countdown every second; "1 more place"; twin places numbered; a guard's key said on the button; the leave item's return told after a dog chase while waiting; the pill dims while the panel can't open; nothing about a rat carried over between games; "Panic all around" (guards don't panic); French: "Il peut l’apporter là-bas", K‑O. never split, "Trop tard, plus personne ne dort !", "Le rat file vers toi", "Plus avant demain", achievements in the tu imperative; the last line clear of the panel's fade; a bed's own icon. 3D: the rat perches on the other shoulder, away from the arm that strikes, eats and punches, and out past the head's corner and a full beard; placed after the raft, the helicopter and the truck have taken the player (no rat left floating), hidden on the title and while crawling out of a vent; floor rats seen from the vents too; a bigger rat on the floor; the trap's matchstick wider, the caught rat's rim at the cup's mouth.
- **P20 third review (v1.14.2)**: three more independent reviews. Rules: no confirmed bug; a dog's rest after a rat chase no longer blocks the player's trail (its own countdown), and a loose rat's darting path stays on the floor too (never the corner of a ducted wall); an inmate stalled on its spot's tile now takes up what it's there for (only what's drawn changes). UI: twin places numbered once and for all by room ("Shower · Showers 4"), in the status and toasts too; T focuses the first Fetch, never Let it loose; only distances changing are written in place (focus and screen reader undisturbed); "brought it back, but your hands are full: at your feet"; "came back with nothing it could carry"; the key said in lower case on the button; aria-keyshortcuts on the pill; French "Pas avant demain", "Trop tard, réveil entre-temps !". 3D: the arm under a perched rat holds still while walking, glances stay within ±0.2 with it there, and it ducks out of sight while the hands are busy (training, eating, reading, showers, strikes, punches, dozing); perched a little further out and lower; a pale rim round its body so it reads on dark floors (grass, carpet) as well as light; feet tucked under it.
- **P21 Smuggling (v1.15.0)**: the burner phone and orders from outside (§2). Sim: `systems/smuggling.ts` after the rats (the drone's drop at its minute, `SALT.DRONE`, its buzz and the guards who come, seizures by sight, the visitor after 3 s seated, `SALT.VISIT` gifts, the Red Cross parcel, the noon frisk; arches in `power.scan`), the visiting day card (`beforeVisits` for old saves and logs), the fixer's `TALK.PHONE`, `EXT.ORDER`, the phone plain to see in hand; ledger `phones`, `orders`, `drops`, `dropsSeized`, `visits`, `smuggled`, `parcelsSeized`; achievements On the Line, Drop Zone, Visiting Hours, Finders Keepers (hidden). Play: the fixer's burner phone in the talk panel (asks twice); the phone's panel (P, Space with it in hand, or the order's pill): the order on its way, the two ways with their fee and when and where each would come, the catalogue with Order buttons that ask twice; the order's pill (its time, the day, "on the ground" pulsing, the visitor's wait as a bar); toasts, the phone's beeps, the drone's buzz and the drop's thump; the warden's visiting-day lines and forecast; rumours (the fixer, where drones drop, the arches, the next visiting day); bubbles (guards at the drone, a guard seizing it, inmates looking forward to their visitors); help topic and key rows. View: a quadcopter flying in from upwind with the parcel slung under it, letting it fall where it lands, blinking lights and spinning rotors; a green beacon over a drop; portable arches at the visiting room's ways out; visitors sat at the tables (instanced, tinted), the player's across from them. Escape bots unchanged.
- **P21 review (in v1.15.0)**: three independent reviews before the release (rules with probes, UI and text in the browser at the three sizes in both languages, 3D measured). Rules: the visiting room's arches came from the tiles as they are (a wall chipped into the room made a game loaded play differently from one played straight on): now from the map as built, and none where a detector arch stands already (Irongate's, Shankton's); the Red Cross parcel went into the desk's hidden compartment (safe from every shakedown): now its open slots; a damaged save's order or drop is dropped, never a crash; Shankton's drop area is the roll call square alone (a drop in the yard beside it was named as roll call), and smuggletest checks every drop area is one zone; where a full pile sends it, the drone's order lands inside the drop area, and the parcel falls there too; no order (nor money taken) a way nothing comes in the weeks ahead; "today" for a visitor only while there's still time to sit the visit; the noon scan never twice. UI: "the visiting room" (« le parloir » without a « de le »), counters or tables, "order things in from outside" (a parcel at Stalag Flucht), trust rather than friendship for the fixer's 60, French agreements round the item (« le colis du drone : … »), the pill's label without a double stop, "Day 6", the help says the pill tells when and the panel where, "Payer 90 $ ?" fits its button, "From the fixer". 3D: only the drone's lights glow and blink (red to port), it keeps its heading and leans with its speed, from 45 tiles off; the portable arches' lamps are the prison's arches' (dark without power, red on a trip) and see-through; visitors' forearms reach the counter and table, the same visitors all morning wherever the player sits, the player's own staying at that table after the visit, settling into the seat as they arrive; nothing allocated a frame; the buzz unheard below ground.
- **P21 second review (v1.15.1)**: three more independent reviews (rules with probes, UI and text in the browser at the three sizes in both languages, 3D measured); every first-review fix held. Rules: standing in an arch at noon scanned the player twice (the noon scan now skips the arches: they scanned on the way in); a drone landing in a guard's sight was seized the instant it fell (now it turns back for another night, as for the sirens: "the drone saw Officer … watching", the help says so); the drone's buzz counted as the player's decoy (Decoy and the noise tip are the player's own noise); San Pancho's canteen arches stood just inside the workshop doors' fixed arches (a key through cost two scans: none now where a fixed arch stands a door beyond); a save's drop with nothing lying there counted as picked up (forgotten now); a visitor "today" only while three minutes are left. UI: the order pill only tells (no phone to open, no "Your order") for a visitor with nothing ordered; the pill says "now" once its time has come; ask-twice buttons say their question to a screen reader; Order and close 40 px on touch; the stay-seated tip short; the panel opens on a way something can come by; "+20 $" in French; disabled Order buttons described by the note; "The Call button"; « Commande passée », « le parloir, ou la cantine », « de passer te voir »; "after dark". 3D: no visitor drawn on the player's own seat nor where an inmate sits still; the visitors the same all morning, the one across from the player's seat being theirs; a drone turned back flies off with its parcel still slung; visitors and arches seen from the vents too (the buzz heard only from the ground floor and the roofs); drawn a step behind like everyone; the phone's panel worked out only while it's open, the pill once a step.
- **P22 Side games (v1.16.0)**: dice, arm-wrestling, sports day and the lockpick (§2). Sim: `systems/games.ts` after training (the games' hours, a bout's push and shove, a lock being picked, a door picked open), talk ops through `social.ts` (a talk switched or ended settles the dice), the lockpick a tool action in `structure.ts`, `World.pickedDoor` derived in `updateDoors`; ledger `dice`, `diceWon`, `bigPots`, `snakeEyes`, `bouts`, `boutsWon`, `champion`, `locksPicked`, `slips`; achievements High Roller, Iron Arm, Open Sesame, Champion, Snake Eyes (hidden). Play: the talk panel's Games (stakes asking twice, the dice chosen to throw again, both throws and the verdict once the inmate's dice are down; the HUD's cash waits for them too), the arm-wrestle card (the tug, the bell, the surge, pads on touch), the lockpick card (the pins, the sweep and its green, a big Set pad, the ear and eye warnings, read to screen readers), cards docked left in landscape; 3D dice tumbling from the hand onto the thrown faces, the two wrestlers square at arm's length with their grips meeting, the player leaning in to a lock, bunting strung on poles over the open yard; the warden's sports-day lines; rumours (the strongest arm, gambling's heat, the lockpick, the next sports day); bubbles. Three reviews folded in before the release: rules (the stake now held on the table: spending mid-game made a loss cheaper; a talk switched left the dice unsettled; the games' hours end a game; the inmate throws again on a tie: the player's edge 6.5% → 4.6%), UI and text (tips over the cards, the cash shown before the reveal, help on opinion, sports-day wording, French and plurals, focus and screen readers), 3D (hands that never met, a lock out of reach, bunting through roofs and trees and ending in mid-air, dice from inside the chest, poses snapping out, reduced motion dropping the poses). Escape and stowaway bots unchanged.
- **P22 second review (v1.16.1)**: three more independent reviews (rules with a 40-run fuzz and 2,202 save/load twins, UI and text in the browser at the three sizes in both languages, 3D measured); every first-review fix held. Rules: dice now one die thrown again at most (the player's edge with best play was +4.6%; the inmate now keeps 2%), dice winnings no longer counted as cash earned (Moneybags), the crowd's cheer once a day per inmate (`state.cheered` appended), no games with an inmate sat down, the sirens said as the reason, the inmate's push back (Strength + 30)/10, a bout's win/loss toast says what actually changed (no "+1 Strength" at 100), picking stops when sat down. UI: the stake shown on the table and the money's floats matching the HUD, Space and focus in the talk panel, the bet's asking twice kept across redraws, a bout broken off said, the lock card's slips and pins read to screen readers, the pads pressable from the keyboard, the wrestle meter's value said, the landscape Set pad no longer moved by the card's animations, wording and line breaks. 3D: bunting in every prison (Stalag Flucht and Shankton had none: the best stretches of open yard in any row or column, near the middle, poles every 14 tiles, never in a bush), the player stands at the lock's face (no longer leaning through a door they stopped against), the grip at one height under strain, the left hand braced on the hip, dice from the hand and clear of the camera's line, the inmate shaking its dice and flinging them, wrestlers meeting at any talk distance.
- **P23 Crew & specialists (v1.17.0)**: every inmate has a line of work and a follower takes orders in it (§2). Sim: `systems/crew.ts` after the side games (whistles, brawls, fetches, the shaft, Lights Out and 06:00), orders through `social.ts`, the digger's help in `actions.ts` strikes, the muscle's in `fights.ts` and `grudges`, the crew's targets before the followers' in `socialNow`, `crewEscape` at the way out; ledger `crewOrders`, `whistles`, `brawls`, `crewFetched`, `dirtTaken`, `ambushesStopped`, `nightsOut`; achievements In Good Company, The Whole Gang, Eyes Peeled, Diversion, Legwork, Many Hands; records keep the most crew out per prison. Play: the talk panel's crew section (each order with what it does or why not, from the sim's `cantOrder`; a brawl asks twice; the night toggle), the crew's pill and panel with a whistle back (G), a chip over each one's head (their line, lit on an order, ringing at a whistle) in place of the follower badge, toasts, a lip whistle and a scuffle, the victory card's line; lookout (hand at the brow, head sweeping) and brawl (fists up, jabs) poses, limbs now easing between activities; rumours on who's good at what; bubbles (orders taken, whistles, brawls and their crowd, a drop handed over, an ambush called off). Three reviews folded in before the release: rules (a lookout in the player's cell doorway or a work room held its door open all night: posts refused there, and the crew kept out of the locked cell; plain followers left exactly as they were for old logs; a drop doubled when the player took it the step the smuggler arrived; "Follow me" lost a drop in hand; the night toggle and re-posts farmed counts; "thinks better of it" repeated every 20 s), UI and text (focus lost on a greyed button, the night's note and its calling off, the pill under side panels, G closing panels with no crew, notes lined up, item names mid-sentence, screen-reader colon and night), 3D (the lookout's hand inside his head, brawlers nose to nose, chips over the follower badge, poses snapping off, allocations every frame). Escape and stowaway bots unchanged.
- **P23 crew card polish (v1.17.1)**: on the user's screenshots the orders read ragged (buttons of different widths, notes wrapping beside them). Each order is now one full-width action on two lines (an icon tile, its name, then what it does or why not now), the order in force lit with an ON tag, tonight's the night keeping its name and saying how to call it off; the same at the three sizes and in French.
- **Prisoner's file polish (v1.17.2)**: on the user's screenshot the job, favors, followers and rumors were plain lines under the attributes' tiled rows. They now share one row look with the attributes: a coloured tile, a title, a line under it and a tag at the end (the job's quota, a favor's reward and state, each follower's line of work and what he's at with a Tonight tag, the dogs' trust), a muted dashed row saying what would go there when a section is empty, and rumors as gold tiles with who told them and when under the line.
- **Prisoner's file cards (v1.17.3)**: the attributes and the rumors take the same card as the job, favors and crew (background, 14 px radius, padding, 32 px tile, 6 px between cards, the same title and line sizes), so every section of the file lines up.
- **P23 second review (v1.17.4)**: three more independent reviews (rules with a 195-twin save/load fuzz, UI and text in the browser at the three sizes in both languages, 3D measured); every first-review fix held but one. Rules: tonight's the night called off after Lights Out left the follower in the player's cell (its door unlocked) and never sent him to bed (now to bed at once); posting again after a recall farmed whistles (a fresh post knows the guards already in sight); the night's company went back to bed while the player was in the vents or a tunnel (now he holds where he is); orders at night to a follower not staying the night were taken then dropped (now refused: tonight's the night first; "Follow me" to a plain follower changes nothing); a post blocked since brings the lookout back; the crew slips out only with the player in sight. UI: the focus after an order stays in the crew's orders (not a dice bet), the file no longer rebuilt every second in a brawl (the countdown written in place, the close button keeping the focus), every card's text starting at one place (the Today rows and the attributes take the cards' tile and gap), the night toggle's pressed state said either way, French distances "à moins de", old rumors struck through on their line only and said so to screen readers, the brawl's question naming who, the pill's night-only line, the crew panel's tiles in orange, the orders still under reduced motion, the panels' crew lists worked out once a step. 3D: followers and brawlers no longer walk on the spot at arm's length (a stalled step stands still: the brawl's pose now shows), the lookout sweeps his whole body (the raised arm clipped the head as it turned) with the arm a little out, the activity blend eases height and lean too (no stand-up pop) and never mutes a blow, the chips scale with the zoom like the badges and sit just clear of a badge beside them, under the speech bubbles.
- **P24 Bluff & glider (v1.18.0)**: two more ways out (§2). Sim: `systems/getaway.ts`, the glide in `structure.ts` and `actions.ts`, the checkpoint's gates in `World` (for the player alone; Irongate's drawbridge too), `Sim.out` names `bluff` and `glider`; state `waved`; ledger `waved`, `glides`, `glideFar`, `glidesIn`; forced days take a wind. Achievements Papers, Please; Wing It; Tailwind; Grounded (hidden). Medal rows for each new route. Play: Show your ID at the window (or why not: the shift change, the uniform, the heat), the green chip while the gates are open, toasts and sounds (a stamp and the gate's buzz, the rush of air, a touchdown); the glider's button says how far and whether out, a dotted path and a ring show it; input waits while the glide is in the air; the escape camera waits for the landing. Rumours (the shift change, a fake ID's recipe, a glider's day: flown for real from a copy), bubbles for a glide seen, help topics The bluff and The glider.
- **P24 review (in v1.18.0)**: three independent reviews before the release (rules with repro scripts, UI and text at the three sizes in both languages, 3D measured). Rules: off Irongate's lowered drawbridge onto the rock outside the wall, the gate shut and the player was stranded for good (ground only the checkpoint leads back from now holds the gates open: `World.pastCheckpoint`, Shankton's gatehouse too); a glide could come down on someone (never now: the tile before, as the button says); the papers took over Interact at the window all day (now first only at the shift change, else when nothing else is in reach). UI: on touch the glide's button said only "Glide" (a tag beside it says how far and whether out); "1 tiles"; French spaces in « », 40 %, 60 s; a refused bluff now greyed; a button that did nothing once waved through gone; the help says the glider is lost landing inside; the old "Four ways out" title. 3D: the frame crossed the sail (now along its edges), the pilot under the bar (the bar at their raised hands), the wreck in the fence (back toward the roof), the roofs vanishing at take-off (drawn until the glide drops below them), the camera between the player and the landing (it follows the flight, and leans out to the ring of a long glide), the wing's pitch rolling it on east and west headings, the preview path above the flight's; the gatekeeper merged into his booth (a draw call fewer). Irongate's draw calls were over 200 before P24 (217–223), noted.
- **Tutorial, chapter 2 "Prison life" (v1.19.0)**: the user asked for the tutorial to teach what the game has gained; P25 was put off for it (2026-10-18). The tutorial in chapters (§2); *Prison life*: Guards' ways (Officer Pratt, corrupt, known from the start; lose the paperwork), The day (the warden's morning, a forced fire drill, roll call in time), Guard dogs (Biscuit sniffs the cutters; a jerky; trust 60 lets them by), The pet rat (fed twice, sent to fetch from Sparky's desk), Smuggling (the burner phone, a battery by drone, picked up before a guard sees it), Side games (dice, then an arm-wrestle, in the evening's free time), Your crew (Sparky recruited and posted as lookout; he whistles as Bell comes by). Reviewed by three independent reviews before the release: rules (four dead ends: at roll call before the bell the muster was lost with the bell's event, a drop picked up from beside it, the jerky thrown before the sniff, the heat cooling off before the bribe; games closing at noon; the rat's food or the rat gone), UI and text (the landscape list's two columns per chapter, time to read the warden's announcement, "announcements" not "board", the dogs lesson's last step shown at least 4 s, French wording: combinard, gardien, suspicion, no-break spaces, the Call and Wrestle buttons named as on screen; a phone's order slipping to tomorrow night while the panel was read, now an hour earlier and said so if late), 3D (Bell walked through the lying dog: the kennel in the hall's north-east corner, in view of the camera).
- **Tutorial, chapter 3 "Ways out" (v1.20.0)**: Picking locks (the purple door, 4 pins, the card's marker and Set; it shuts again before long), Stowaway (delivery day forced at Center Perks on a seed whose search is a quick look; hide before the 14:25 sign-off; away at 14:31, the truck driving off before the card), The bluff (in uniform with a fake ID at the gatekeeper's window from 19:34; shown at the 19:50–20:10 shift change; out through the gates opened for a minute), The glider (on Center Perks' roof at 23:20 with a west wind and a dummy in bed; the dots, the ring and the button's distance; off the east edge out to the road). Floor arrows show on the roof in a lesson (a step names its layer; elsewhere nothing changes). Reviewed by three independent reviews before the release: rules (the gates shut again and the door shut again were dead ends; hiding is only possible until the 14:25 sign-off, not 14:31; the bed check's lockdown on the roof; lesson arrows on another layer than the goal's), UI and text (French « portail » not « grille », « cercle » not « anneau », "the outer gates", a door that doesn't stay open long, more time before the shift change), 3D (the truck never drove off in a lesson; a moment on the road after the glide).
- **Tutorial, chapter "Getting by" (v1.21.0)**, placed second in the list: Friends and favors (Training Block: a gift, Sparky's favor taken on and handed in, rumors from friends), Merchants (Center Perks' stall in free time), A job (the laundry: basket, washer, clean basket, the quota), The gym (a set on the weights), The disguise (in uniform past Bell at night), Tunnels (the entrance under the desk, its dirt flushed, down through the desk, braces after every two tiles, up past the fence), Vents and roofs (Center Perks after Lights Out with a dummy in bed: the cell's grate, the ducts, the ladder to the roof). The tutorial now has 28 lessons in four chapters. Reviewed by three independent reviews before the release: rules (by day a guard sweeping the cells saw the open grate and called a lockdown: now after Lights Out; the tunnel's dig step ticked on any return to the ground; digging up takes a press a strike; braces after every two tiles, a third one spare; the job's arrows), UI and text (the tunnel's start diagonal to the desk dug beside it, and the desk's ring on its chair: below the desk now; touch texts for the job's stations named as on screen; rumors, Work period, the gift's two taps), 3D (the exit hole against the fence: out three tiles past it).
- **Dig up, its own control (v1.22.0)**, after the user found no way to dig up from a tunnel (it only came at the tunnel's end, turned to face back along it, on Space): in a tunnel a Dig up button beside the tool button (touch) or U (keyboard) strikes up through the floor overhead whichever way the player faces, held to strike on, wherever the floor can be dug through (never a wall, the fence, furniture, someone, the entrance or the desk); grey without a digging tool; an ear when a guard would hear. A new command (`EXT.DIG_UP`); the old way stays in the sim, so old replays and the escape bots play as before (escapetest unchanged). The tunnel lesson, the hints and the help say it; the lesson ends (try again) only on digging up inside the fence, and any side of the fence counts as out. Reviewed: rules (a hole dug down by mistake, or coming up past the side fence, read as "dug up inside": now the dig-up itself and where it lands), UI and text (the hint, toasts and the action button's tag over the new control on desktop, portrait and landscape; the ear badge; French "Percer").
- **A direction with no compass point (v1.22.1)**, found in the v1.22.0 rules review: a command with direction 17–31 (never sent by the controls or bots, only by a corrupt or hand-made log) made the player's position NaN. `dirOf` now reads it as standing still; simtest checks it (and fails without the fix). Escapetest unchanged.
- **Tutorial review, and the objectives bulb (v1.23.0)**, after the user found the lesson card sometimes out of sight with the objective forgotten: a bulb beside the pause button in a lesson (and O, and the pause card's Objectives) opens the lesson's objectives over the paused game (the steps done, the one to do now, how many are left; the steps ahead unsaid), with a dot while a step came up the card couldn't show. The card itself stays in sight: banners no longer fade it (on phones they come under it, in landscape beside it), toasts on phones come under it, at a game or a lock in landscape it takes the top row's middle, and a panel over it (a screen too narrow for both) shows the step at its top. A full review of the 28 lessons (four rules reviewers, one per chapter, playing a real player's mistakes headless, and one in the browser) fixed every dead end found: any lesson ends (try again) when the player is shocked by a live fence (caught and knocked out already did) or gets out some other way (done only if every step left holds); roll call missed, the lookout called off, the favor lapsed, the wrong order, a stray tunnel hole, braces spent early (4 now, past the fence at z ≤ 11) no longer leave a lesson waiting for ever; dropped items (firecrackers, the lockpick, the glider) aren't lost; a step with something to read is never ticked past in the same breath (disguise's warning); Space no longer digs up at a tunnel's end facing back (U alone does; the sim rule stays for old replays, escapetest unchanged); a dozen texts say what was missing (he hits back, press quickly, step back from the dog, keep the cutters in a pocket, face the ladder, stay near the yard). Reviewed again before release: rules (false wins on an early escape, roll call skipped in the cell, a favor lapsing to nothing) and UI and text (the strip sticky in a scrolled panel, the top row at 360 px, the banner beside a game card, wording).
- **P26 Daily Escape (v2.0.0)**, on the user's go (2026-10-19; P25 the replay viewer stays put off): a Daily a day, the same for everyone (§2): a prison, a seed and 1–3 of 21 twists to a weekly rhythm and budget, Daily #1 on 19 October 2026; its own save, unlimited tries, yesterday's a day late, the streak, the result card to share (medal, day, tries, strip, check code). The escape bots moved to `scripts/escapes.ts` (escapetest's output unchanged) so dailytest can play each prison's hardest Daily: 28 routes, every twist there at once but those closing them, all out (Lone Wolf and Bare Hands close the takeover, Bare Hands the helicopter, Closed Shop the roof, the raft and the helicopter). Two independent reviews before release (rules with probes; UI and text in the browser at the three sizes in both languages; no 3D change, so no 3D review). Rules: Quick Fix mended the shaft under the player's desk and wiped the tunnels' digging (now only what a guard could see, and ground-floor wear but under the desk); Big Freeze dealt at prisons with heatwaves (no heatwave or rain under it; its storms are blizzards); the tool button promised a weapon under Bare Hands, Recruit under Lone Wolf, a bribe under Straight-Laced with chatter off; pairs where one twist made the other moot (Light Sleepers with Straight-Laced, Customs with Truck Strike, Three Days with either); unpaid days counted as wages; the best kept another escape's medal; a Daily's tries counted one escape; a saved run of yesterday's out of reach.
